## GRAND PRIX CHIBA 2015

TOP 8 COVERAGE

Yuki Matsumoto has upgraded on his past performance earlier in 2015. Having earned his first Grand Prix Top 8 in Shizuoka, January 2015, Matsumoto went beyond that performance with his win at the historic Grand Prix Chiba.

Fittingly, the format for Grand Prix Chiba, *Modern Masters 2015 Edition* Limited, covers a span of time that ends with *Scars of Mirrodin*, the set in which Matsumoto first began playing the game. And it was that set that truly powered him through this Top 8. His White-Blue artifact deck contained some powerful synergies as well as brute-force power such as Mirran Crusader, a card that let him turn the corner in his finals match against 2011 World Champion Junya Iyanaga. 

As a player who first started in the modern era of *Magic*, it is too fitting that at the end of the event, it's Yuki Matsumoto who has been crowned Chiba's modern master.

DAY 2 COVERAGE

Grand Prix Chiba's Day 2 action is covered here, and while Makuhari Messa's event hall was filled with Magic players from all over the globe, 379 of those players headed into Day 2 of the Grand Prix Chiba main event. The format for the Day 2 main event is *Modern Masters 2015 Edition* Booster Draft, where main event competitors will participate in two booster drafts before a cut to Top 8.

DAY 1 COVERAGE

The last of the players in Makuhari Messe are headed out after Day 1, many intent on returning for Day 2 to continue living *Magic* through this Modern Masters Weekend. 

The final tally of main event participants for Day 1 at Grand Prix Chiba? 3,551.

The number of players returning for more *Modern Masters 2015 Edition* main event action for Day 2? 380. 

Check out Day 2 coverage to see more stories of greatness from Grand Prix Chiba. Looking for more coverage, check out how Utrecht and Las Vegas players did during the Modern Masters Weekend.
